SK hynix's DRAM prices jumped 30% and NAND roughly 50% quarter-over-quarter, driving the company's margin to a record.

What this means:
Google News RSS item reports Micron's HBM is sold out through 2026 and data centre revenue grew 150%, with MU trading near $914.

内容:
一则 Google News RSS 报道称，Micron 的 HBM 已售罄至 2026 年，数据中心营收增长 150%，MU 股价接近 $914。

Why it matters:
Sold-out HBM through 2026 signals hard supply constraints on the memory feeding AI accelerators, reinforcing the memory-shortage side of the C6 thesis — hyperscalers are competing for constrained HBM capacity, which supports pricing power for Micron but adds another bottleneck/cost pressure to the AI-capex build-out.

What this means:
A Digitimes item says the NAND price rally is plateauing, with supply staying tight even as spot demand softens.

内容:
Digitimes 报道称，NAND 价格涨势进入平台期，尽管现货需求走软，供应仍然紧张。

Why it matters:
Tight NAND supply plus cooling demand is a classic late-cycle memory signal — pricing power holds for now, but a plateau after a rally often precedes a rollover once spot demand keeps weakening. Relevant to the AI hardware supply chain since HBM/NAND tightness has been a key input-cost pressure feeding the capex thesis.

影响:

内存价格紧张（NAND 短缺）与 AI capex 驱动的需求（Google、Vera Rubin）同时出现，正是 C6 信号要捕捉的模式——超大规模厂商的 AI 建设已经明显挤压上游内存供应，这种成本压力可能传导到所有 AI capex 预算中。

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.39% (+56bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-23 22:29 UTC

Date:     2026-07-22
Current:  2.39%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +56bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.37% (+54bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-22 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-21
Current:  2.37%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +54bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.35% (+52bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-21 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-20
Current:  2.35%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +52bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.
